# Laminated maple-shell acoustic snare drum manufacture


## 1. Scope and Applicability

This PCR covers one complete new non-electrical acoustic snare drum with a laminated maple wood shell and actual lacquer finishing, delivered through its first quality release. The foreground includes real supplied plywood preparation, shell glue forming/drying and machining, actual coating, complete supplied heads and metal hardware assembly, snare-wire/adjuster installation, tuning and inspection. A supplier-laminated bare shell is an explicit evidenced make/buy alternative; it does not authorise a purchased finished drum plus packaging boundary. This is a transparent technical synthesis of primary route, anatomy and supplier documents, not a claim that all observations describe the same SKU, order or factory. No layer count, adhesive recipe, density, thickness, tuning force, tooling settings, unit weight, life or performance/certification default is imposed.

Excluded: drum kits, electrical pickups/electronic drums, other wood species or stave/solid/steam-bent/metal shells without an explicit applicability reassessment, brass and string instruments, cymbals/gongs, shell/head/wire-only accessories, toys, stands and installation systems. The practical terminal is a playable assembled snare, not raw timber or a spare head. Actual packaging/storage/handling remains recorded after release with its own original-cohort service account.

## 4. Measurement and Unit Rules

| rule_id | Applies to | Required property | Required unit | Rule |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| release_mass | reference product | Mass | kg | Q is actual net mass summed only over newly first-released complete drums of the declared cohort; use calibrated scales with tare removed and traceable configuration/serial/release, moisture state and included components. Do not infer mass from a webpage, density or secondary native factor. |
| numerator_unit | every inventory numerator | native extensive property | row-specific | Raw collection records original units. Convert each raw mass, energy, volume, count or service-hour observation to that row declared extensive unit before any real burden allocation; never impose Mass/kg on MJ, m3, item or h. |
| electricity_units | electricity; service_electricity | Net calorific value | MJ | Convert measured kWh using 3.6 MJ/kWh only as exact unit conversion; no conversion efficiency or extra electricity procurement. |
| volume_state | maple_plywood; compressed_air | Volume | m3 | Measure actual sheet geometry/volume and state; air volume records pressure/temperature/humidity and declared reference state. Integrated volume already is a total; integrate only true rates once. Any needed kg bridge uses independently measured substance-specific density and state, never water density or native secondary ratios. |
| count_bridge | item-based observations | Number of items | item | Number of items/Item(s) and item denote the same physical count with factor one when genuinely used. For count-to-kg drums or assets independently weigh actual complete configuration/batch with calibrated scale, packaging exclusion and moisture/component scope; no single-item kg or lifetime is assumed. Q remains measured net released mass. |
| single_normalisation | all inventory rows | row-specific numerator / Mass denominator | row unit per kg | Keep all protocol records raw. Prepare the row numerator first; perform the one section 8 quotient once after actual conversion/allocation. Gross cards use full actual V; service uses actual original-cohort increment and that cohort Qc/H; manufacturing uses its release-cohort numerator and Q. Q=0 retains loads without unit result. |
| wet_whole_preparation | supplied wet articles and preparations | Mass | kg | Whole wet delivery includes supplier ingredients/carrier once; separate actual additional onsite ingredients and losses. Full wet closing mass includes actual retained incoming repair material/water; X terms deduplicate other same-side flows only. |
| geometry | actual geometry records | Volume or measured area | m3 or m2 | Product reference is mass, not area. If auxiliary volume/area is needed, use actual measured shape or traceable configuration-specific drawing geometry; L times W only for proven rectangular geometry, no bounding box, double-head area or guessed thickness/density. |

## 5. System Boundary

### Boundary Abstraction

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| declared_starting_condition | Actual supplied flexible maple plywood or explicitly supplier-laminated bare shell plus complete supplied components and actual local preparations; no bought finished-drum packaging shortcut. |
| starting_condition_role | foreground production start; upstream supplied-state burden retained once |
| product_classification_scope | CPC 3.0 38350 context only; this method is narrower complete acoustic laminated maple-shell snare |
| recursive_input_rule | A supplied shell/component uses compatible upstream manufacture once; internal same-product handoffs consolidate to zero; do not treat a whole finished bought drum as raw shell. |
| upstream_dataset_requirement | Match actual whole preparation/component, supplier route/state/material and geographic/temporal scope; reject incompatible hard restrictions and retain missing providers as ordinary collection duties. |
| disclosure | Disclose actual make/buy, process sequence, delivered component boundary, first-release cohort, stock inheritance, service horizon, all gross physical gates, uncertain quantities and all excluded phases. |

The following full rules apply together to the declared first-release boundary and actual conditions; conditional absence requires evidence, never an invented zero. Gross physical ledgers and burden vectors are distinct dimensions.

| rule_id | applies_to | rule | source_ids |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| boundary_route | manufacture | Begin with actual supplied flexible maple plywood, laminating preparation and components; cut sheets, apply actual glue, circular airbag mould, dry, trim, sand/coat/cure/buff, machine bearing edges and snare beds, drill and vent, assemble whole heads/hardware/wire/strainer and tune/inspect. Actual site order may differ and must be recorded. Local microwave and UV operations require their measured electricity; do not create a boiler or steam demand. | `yamaha-shell`; `yamaha-finish`; `yamaha-assembly` |
| boundary_make_buy | shell_bought; maple_plywood | Externally laminated whole bare shell is an explicit alternative, owned upstream once with supplier proof. Replace only those demonstrated outsourced operations, retaining actual local machining/finishing/assembly/test. Internal same-product handoffs cancel at consolidation; no second product procurement or second Q. Raw-log/veneer preparation is upstream of the demonstrated supplied plywood, not invented onsite. If actual onsite veneer preparation occurs, add its individual wood, glue, energy and loss exchanges before use. | `yamaha-shell` |
| boundary_components | all supplied components | Heads include supplied film/coating/retaining rim, plated metal components include supplier plating and supplied screws, and snare kits include their supplied end plates/anchors. Procure each whole delivered preparation/article once. Record supplier composition nonadditively; separate only truly additional purchases or onsite manufacture with individual materials, operations and losses. | `yamaha-components`; `remo-head` |
| boundary_first_release | finished | Q is sum of actual unwrapped net complete snare mass at its first new quality release in one declared configuration/cohort and time window. Released closing FG is already in Q and its manufacturing burden: never subtract it as unreleased inventory or add it as a second output. Opening released FG, reinspection, dispatch and returns do not create Q. No net-delivery denominator is used. | `yamaha-assembly` |
| boundary_burden_stock | all burden-bearing rows | Maintain each exchange inherited burden vector separately from physical stock. For manufacturing numerator use original raw numerator after unit conversion and actual allocation, plus inherited opening raw/WIP/unassigned non-WIP stores, less only closing raw/WIP/unassigned non-WIP burden not yet assigned to release. Never subtract already assigned released FG or already-net consumption stocks again. A legitimate transfer requires identified other cohort/function and justified allocation/handover once; ordinary rejects, samples, sold or exported waste retain manufacture and treatment burden. | `ilcd-2010` |
| boundary_all_actual_work | all processes | Include all actual rejected/zero-output batches, trials, start/stop, idle, cleaning and rework. Q=0 retains measured load and stock vectors without a unit result. Unknown actual quantity is not zero. Permission and posttests/acceptance control authorisation and continued conformance, never whether already performed activity is recorded. | `ilcd-2010`; `yamaha-assembly` |
| boundary_service | service; assets; packaging | Keep actual postrelease storage/handling/packaging/service increments with original cohort first-release mass Qc and observed horizon H; closing FG does not remove performed original-cohort service. Only unused/unassigned service or charge may carry forward. Future unknown service is disclosed and not imputed. Consolidating manufacturing and service cancels internal Q handoff and counts one reference output. | `ilcd-2010` |
| boundary_rework | service | Actual postrelease rework requires manufacturer/specification permission plus postwork tests, acceptance and disposition to assert continued conformance. Unapproved, failed or nonconforming work still retains all actual materials, water, energy, old/new parts, original cohort and hold/waste disposition. Do not presume damaged shell/head recovery. Natural storage humidity uptake or evaporation is not a repair and needs no fictional permission. | `yamaha-assembly`; `ilcd-2010` |
| boundary_physical_gates | all physical legs | Reconcile manufacturing, FG/service and consolidated physical gates before any burden share. Complete gross legs and full wet opening/closing mass stay intact; all repair-material/water and packaging X_in/X_out terms only remove duplicated OTHER same-side incoming/outgoing terms. A real influx retained in closing stock remains both influx and full closing mass. Internal reuse cancels within the consolidated gate, not as new procurement or production. | `ilcd-2010` |
| boundary_packaging | packaging | For each actual carton/PE bag/paper pad/PET strap/wood pallet pool collect six separate physical events P/I/ER/S/EO/W: opening empty plus attached WIP/FG plus reusable stock + P + I + ER = closing same full stock + gross shipped S + external owner return EO + waste W, with actual repair/humidity input/output and other genuine transformations added disjointly. P and I are mutually exclusive ownership of receipt; first non-purchased external ingress I is still real physical ingress with one upstream owner. Whole wet P/I once, disjoint article/component bridge. | `ilcd-2010` |
| boundary_waste_outlet | all waste rows | Six pool directions are measured even when wastes share one outlet. If W sources physically co-collect, retain each source W measurement as a nonadditive subaccount of the one actual mixed Waste exchange; never sum mixed total plus components or book treatment twice. Only genuinely separate outlets use separate external Waste exchanges. Sale/export is not automatic allocation relief or substitution credit. | `ilcd-2010` |
| boundary_assets | assets | Separate complete new/used physical assets and replacement parts/fluids from current service burden. Record gross acquisition, returns, retirement, opening/closing installed assets/charge, transfers, real maintenance and disposal. Measure actual production/maintenance burden and observed service allocation independently; no default lifetime, kg-per-item factor or one machine per kg. Asset gross movements never shrink by service/economic share. | `ilcd-2010` |
| boundary_heat_water | heat; thermal_water_in; thermal_water_return | Only actual supplied heat is counted. Heat MJ and actual liquid heat-carrier kg/stock/return are separate; record state, inlet/outlet temperature/enthalpy and one supplier water-energy convention to avoid double purchasing whole heat provider and its carrier energy. A genuine returned carrier is Product return, not waste; no invented steam, condensate, boiler or heat circuit. | `ilcd-2010` |
| boundary_additions | all processes | Before modelling any additional actually used wood species, veneer, adhesive, formulation component separately bought, gas, liquid phase, packaging pool, maintenance item, treatment or freight, add its own atomic card, precise measurement and supplier/identity review. Do not hide real flows in other, zero or an aggregate; split onsite manufacture into real independent raw inputs/energy/loss and cancel internal handoffs. | `ilcd-2010`; `yamaha-shell` |
| packaging_receipt_basis | packaging | For each actual whole packaging article, P records paid procurement (standalone or bundled, with or without a separate invoice). I records first non-purchased external ingress, including standalone gifts, loans, rental/pool issues and unpaid supplier-attached packaging; supplier attachment is only one I subset. ER requires the same item or an auditable matching batch linked to this site's own prior outward record; another party's prior use alone is not a return. P/I/ER are disjoint. The existing matching physical-article cards and raw subledgers cover these origins subject to every native material/form/state qualification; incompatible actual articles require their own atomic rows, never a substitute UUID. X_in/X_out remain separate measured material/water-change terms, not a substitute for whole-article receipt. Keep gross wet quantities, owner/custodian, payment/non-purchase basis, provenance, prior-outward links, actual state/unit and inherited manufacture/service vectors. A free receipt is neither a new manufacture nor automatically burden-free: attribute actual service and incremental transport/handling once to the proper original cohort/denominator and retain genuinely unused service separately, without a default lifetime or credit. Stock, physical gross legs and burden vectors remain separate. This is the declared conserving foreground-accounting implementation, not a claim that the cited source prescribes these variable names. | ilcd-2010 |

## 6. Process Inventory Structure

### Process Map

| process_id | process_name | inclusion | inclusion_condition | role | quantitative_reference |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| manufacture | Shell preparation through new quality release | required | All actual operations and failed work in declared route | Manufacturing release | finished |
| service | Original-cohort postrelease services | conditional | All actual operations and failed work in declared route | Physical gross events and separately attributable burden | original-cohort reference scale only; no second Q |
| packaging | Actual packaging physical event pools | conditional | All actual operations and failed work in declared route | Physical gross events and separately attributable burden | original-cohort reference scale only; no second Q |
| assets | Actual assets and attributed production service | conditional | All actual operations and failed work in declared route | Physical gross events and separately attributable burden | original-cohort reference scale only; no second Q |

Each card is one actual event, not authority to manufacture its occurrence. Whole preparations are purchased once. External-model exchanges must deduplicate mixed-outlet and internal-handoff cards as explicitly stated. A measured source W subaccount in a single mixed outlet does not create a second Waste exchange. The sole quotient is single_quotient in section 8; card amount text never asks collection to divide first. The gross-card result is a physical disclosure only, not a second environmental purchase.

### Process: Shell preparation through new quality release (`manufacture`)

#### Inputs

##### Product flows

###### Maple plywood sheet (`maple_plywood`)

Purchased thin maple plywood sheet in the actual supplied ply construction. Measure actual sheet volume and independently weigh the complete bonded sheet for physical mass balance; included veneer and supplier adhesive are constituent subaccounts, not additional purchases. Collect trimming, moisture and rejected blanks. Native Product is semi-finished plywood manufactured by veneer gluing and pressing, production mix at plant, measured as Volume/m3. No fixed CAS, grade or geography is given. The description defines crossed glued wood veneers and mentions MDF/OSB only as other members of the engineered-board family; those are not this flow. Its bamboo taxonomy label conflicts with the physically generic wood-plywood definition and is disclosed as a classification defect, not taken as proof of bamboo material. Qualify actual flexible maple sheets, supplied ply/glue boundary and measured sheet volume; do not use bamboo, MDF, OSB, raw veneer or finished shell. No native factor establishes density. The named single_quotient in section 8 is applied only once after raw collection. Separate original-cohort service and manufacturing burdens before constructing numerator A; do not reduce actual physical gross legs by burden shares.

- Selected flow: Plywood `8b239d58-5fc2-40a5-8003-33f4082bc995`
- Flow property / unit: Volume `93a60a56-a3c8-22da-a746-0800200c9a66` / m3
- Amount rule: A / Q once after raw conversion and actual attribution; Q = this row original release-cohort kg; see complete section 8 numerator conditions
- Value mode: Calculated value (`calculated_value`)
- Specificity: Site-specific (`site_specific`)
- Normalization basis: per reference flow
- Basis kind: Process output (`process_output`)
- Evidence kind: Calculated from collection (`calculated_from_collection`)
- Collection protocol: `cp_maple_plywood`
- Sources: `yamaha-shell`

###### Electricity (`service_electricity`)

Actual incremental storage, handling, packaging, inspection and postrelease work electricity, even when FG is held or rework unapproved/failed; retain original cohort and observation horizon. Native Product is alternating current below 1 kV, CN location of supply, user-side consumption mix and grid-average supply including production/transmission to user. Bind only a compatible measured CN low-voltage supply and provider boundary, not high-voltage or renewable-certificate-only supply. Reference property is Net calorific value/MJ; convert a measured kWh register by the exact unit identity 1 kWh = 3.6 MJ without inventing an efficiency. No duplicate generation/transmission already inside one supply. The named single_quotient in section 8 is applied only once after raw collection. ##### Product flows

###### Complete acoustic maple-shell snare drum dispatched (`fg_dispatch`)

Actual gross physical dispatch of the same finished cohort; 1 kg reference remains first release, not net sales. Keep finished stock, returns and original release identity. No second Q, manufacture procurement or economic scaling of physical mass. Native scope is the broad finished manufactured percussion-instrument Product family, production mix at plant, with no fixed CAS or geographic/material-grade restriction. This row is restricted to the complete non-electrical acoustic laminated maple-shell snare and the physical event stated above; not a component, toy, electronic instrument or another sound-producing mechanism. Supplier compatibility is independently required. The named single_quotient in section 8 is applied only once after raw collection. For this physical disclosure numerator A is the actual complete gross leg V in this row native unit; it is never a stock-corrected or economically allocated burden vector and creates no purchase, provider burden or credit. Any true attributable burden is assigned separately once.

##### Elementary flows

###### Water vapour (`service_water_evap`)

Actual vapour loss from drying, curing or natural service humidity exchange to air. Separate free, reaction and structural water from evidence; vapour condensate to treatment is a different measured liquid stream, not another copy of this exit. Native Elementary water vapour H2O CAS 7732-18-5, Mass/kg, emission to air unspecified. Only actual gaseous water leaving this boundary; the native broad synonym list includes ice, liquid, heavy/tritiated water and bound-water labels but does not make those states or isotopes equivalent. Not liquid wastewater, product heat-carrier return or a second copy of condensate. No fixed grade/geography. The named single_quotient in section 8 is applied only once after raw collection. Separate original-cohort service and manufacturing burdens before constructing numerator A; do not reduce actual physical gross legs by burden shares.

Retained material stays in closing full wet pallet stock; X removes only overlap with another same-side flow. The named single_quotient in section 8 is applied only once after raw collection. For this physical disclosure numerator A is the actual complete gross leg V in this row native unit; it is never a stock-corrected or economically allocated burden vector and creates no purchase, provider burden or credit. Any true attributable burden is assigned separately once.

- Selected flow: Steel pallet repair nail

- Amount rule: A / Q once; A = actual complete gross leg V in this row native unit; Q = selected release-cohort kg; physical disclosure only

- Specificity: Site-specific (`site_specific`)

### Process: Actual assets and attributed production service (`assets`)

#### Inputs

##### Product flows

###### Woodworking sawing machine (`saw_receipt`)

actual new or used asset received. Preserve complete gross physical count and independent kg bridge, serial/configuration, opening/closing and supplier ownership; never multiply these physical legs by service or economic shares. Do not purchase one machine per kg of drum. For a mixed retirement outlet retain component subaccounts without duplicate exit. The named single_quotient in section 8 is applied only once after raw collection. For this physical disclosure numerator A is the actual complete gross leg V in this row native unit; it is never a stock-corrected or economically allocated burden vector and creates no purchase, provider burden or credit. Any true attributable burden is assigned separately once.

- Selected flow: Woodworking sawing machine
- Flow property / unit: Number of items / item
- Amount rule: A / Q once; A = actual complete gross leg V in this row native unit; Q = selected release-cohort kg; physical disclosure only
- Value mode: Calculated value (`calculated_value`)
- Specificity: Site-specific (`site_specific`)
- Normalization basis: per reference flow
- Basis kind: Process output (`process_output`)
- Evidence kind: Calculated from collection (`calculated_from_collection`)
- Collection protocol: `cp_saw_receipt`
- Sources: `yamaha-shell`; `yamaha-finish`; `yamaha-assembly`

###### Woodworking sawing machine production service (`saw_service`)

Actual service burden of this one asset in observed operating hours, including start/stop, idle, failure, trial and cleanup. Calibrated service records and real asset production/maintenance burden determine allocation; no fixed life or annual utilization. Previously performed service stays with its original cohort; carry forward only unused/unassigned service or charge. Gross assets remain independent. The named single_quotient in section 8 is applied only once after raw collection. Separate original-cohort service and manufacturing burdens before constructing numerator A; do not reduce actual physical gross legs by burden shares.

- Selected flow: Woodworking sawing machine production service
- Flow property / unit: Service duration / h
- Amount rule: A / Q once after raw conversion and actual attribution; Q = this row original release-cohort kg; see complete section 8 numerator conditions
- Value mode: Calculated value (`calculated_value`)
- Specificity: Site-specific (`site_specific`)
- Normalization basis: per reference flow
- Basis kind: Process output (`process_output`)
- Evidence kind: Calculated from collection (`calculated_from_collection`)
- Collection protocol: `cp_saw_service`
- Sources: `yamaha-shell`; `yamaha-finish`; `yamaha-assembly`

##### Elementary flows

## 7. Allocation and Co-product Handling

The hierarchy below applies only within the stated attributional scenario and after separating actual dedicated work. Instrument manufacture evidence supports the route, not allocation coefficients.

| rule_id | applies_to | rule | source_ids |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| allocation_context | all burden-bearing exchanges | This is attributional modelling when allocation is necessary. First isolate exclusive operations and virtual subdivisions; then determine the physical causality that controls each remaining exchange. Only a demonstrated residual lacking such causality may use actual contemporaneous market value at the common process gate and actual state/quality. Document the residual and sensitivity; never choose physical/economic freely or impose a fixed percentage. | `ilcd-2010` |
| allocation_service | service and capital burden | For shared service consider the actual limiting relation and observed duration jointly where appropriate: airbag/microwave/UV machine work by real measured operating relationship; storage/transport by occupied volume/area or limiting mass and time. Collect independent determinants and traceable service burden, rather than assuming one driver fits every exchange. Already performed original-cohort service is not deferred because the drum remains in FG. | `ilcd-2010` |
| allocation_no_credit | waste; rejects; samples; internal reuse | Ordinary waste, failed drums, samples and sold residues remain with manufacture/treatment. Only a real different function/cohort plus justified allocation/handover permits one burden-vector transfer, while gross physical masses stay complete. Internal reuse is neither second procurement nor automatic co-product/avoided production. | `ilcd-2010` |
| allocation_gross | all physical disclosure cards | Gross cards use A = actual complete gross V and the sole quotient for disclosure scale only. They are not upstream purchases, credits, economic shares or a second allocation. Attribute any actual procurement/treatment/service burden once to its actual owner separately. Packaging P/I is one owner, ER/reuse no automatic virgin purchase; export W one actual outlet/provider. | `ilcd-2010` |

## 8. Foreground Data Collection, Calculation, and Quality Rules

Raw protocol observations retain original units and are never divided by Q in collection. The aggregation_rule column declares the one final reporting target only; section 8 prepares the proper numerator and executes single_quotient once. For gross cards A is complete actual V, whereas environmental burden dimensions are independently assigned once. No intermediate card or aggregation may normalise again.

### Data Collection Protocols

| protocol_id | process_id | flow_role | record_type | raw_fields | collection_method | unit | frequency | temporal_coverage | site_scope | aggregation_rule | quality_evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| cp_maple_plywood | manufacture | maple_plywood | collected_record | timestamp; site; actual operation; operator; cohort; configuration; flow identity; direction; original value; original unit; actual state; calibration; actual condition or evidence of absence; full opening stock; gross receipts; gross exits; full closing stock; source and included-component ownership; inherited exchange-specific burden vector; original-cohort first-release kg; observed horizon; uncertainty; raw numerator; dedicated and shared operation driver; raw/WIP/unassigned store inheritance; already-net flag; actual allocation justification; no second stock correction | Collect unnormalised actual maple plywood sheet observations by calibrated weighing/counting or traceable dedicated meters and supplier/acceptance/stock records, specific to this one physical event. ### Calculation Rules

| rule_id | Applies to | Formula or rule | Inputs | Output | source_ids |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| single_quotient | all inventory rows | x = A / Q. First convert original raw observations to the row native unit; apply real burden allocation only to burden dimensions. For manufacturing A is original attributable raw numerator plus inherited opening raw/WIP/unassigned non-WIP burden minus only unassigned closing raw/WIP/non-WIP burden and once-justified true other-cohort/function handover. Already-net raw consumption gets no second stock correction; assigned released closing FG is never subtracted. For actual performed service A is original-cohort observed increment after only legitimate unused/unassigned service/charge carryover, using that cohort first-release mass Qc and horizon H. For every gross disclosure A equals complete actual gross leg V, with no stock correction or economic share and no new procurement/provider/credit. Q is corresponding actual first-release kg (Qc for original-cohort service); Q=0 retains raw/load without x. x is applied once; reference finished output is exactly 1 kg. | raw; native_unit; actual_allocation; inherited_vectors; V; Q; Qc; H | x | `ilcd-2010` |
| physical_balance | all physical gates | opening wet mass + complete actual gross ingress = closing wet mass + complete actual gross egress, with evidenced internal chemical transformations and each true water/species bridge. X terms remove only duplicated OTHER same-side flows; retained new repair material or water remains influx plus full closing wet stock. Reconcile related elements independently; never solve two unknowns from one residual. | opening_wet; incoming_gross; outgoing_gross; closing_wet; species; same_side_overlap | physical_reconciliation | `ilcd-2010` |
| packaging_balance | packaging | For each actual packaging pool separately: opening full empty/attached-WIP-FG/reusable stock + P + I + ER + independent actual repair/water ingress = closing full wet same stock + gross S + EO + W + independent real repair/water egress. P/I whole article once; component bridge disjoint; internal reuse cancels. Each W source in one mixed outlet remains a nonadditive subaccount, not another waste/treatment. | opening_pool; P; I; ER; S; EO; W; repair_in; repair_out; moisture_in; moisture_out; closing_pool | packaging_reconciliation | `ilcd-2010` |
| unit_preparation | raw numerators | Use exact dimensional conversion for recorded units; measured kWh times 3.6 gives MJ. True rates are integrated over their actual interval once; already integrated gas/liquid volumes are not multiplied by time. Volume-to-mass uses independently measured density for that material/state; count-to-mass uses actual calibrated same-configuration batch mass. No provider/energy duplication or invented density. | original_value; original_unit; measured_density; actual_state; calibrated_batch_mass; interval | native_unit_raw | `ilcd-2010` |

### Data Quality Requirements

| requirement_id | Applies to | Requirement | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| dq_identity | all rows | Whole drum/component/formulation/species identity and real supplier condition; every adopted UUID limited by complete native card context. | Supplier specification; actual BOM; primary route and exact native identity |
| dq_measurement | all protocols | Raw data, calibration, original units, actual state/density/geometry and unique release cohort; no imputed values. | Meter/scale logs; calibration; release register; uncertainties |
| dq_complete | all gates | All actual failed/Q0 and performed operations, full gross wet stocks and source subaccounts; absence demonstrated. | Stock reconciliation; raw/WIP inherited vectors; outlet and maintenance records |
| dq_temporal | service and manufacture | Separate manufacture window from each original-cohort observed service horizon and unknown future. | Cohort/date/horizon; original serial delivery and return links |
| dq_allocation | burden vectors | Exclusive subdivision then per-exchange determining physical causality; residual market value only justified common-gate actual state. | Dedicated meters; drivers; actual prices; sensitivity; transfer ownership |
| dq_sources | method and quantitative evidence | Full source context and contrary alternatives, independence by publication not registration count; no industrial page as allocation evidence and no identity result as performance proof. | Source sections; supplier data; limitations; actual approval/disposition |

## 9. Validation Rules

These checks cover the full declared method together with complete section context; an unknown or unsupported real relationship remains explicit review. A mechanical check cannot close physical or scientific uncertainty.

| rule_id | applies_to | rule | source_ids |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| validation_core | finished | Require the full whole acoustic snare terminal, exact Product reference chain and primary-qualified material/route/components; a part, raw wood or wrong sound-producing family cannot stand for the drum. Broader but physically compatible manufactured percussion family is allowed with the complete restrictions. Missing real core identity or route is a true hold, draft/unusable and unmapped. | `yamaha-components`; `yamaha-assembly` |
| validation_first_release | all collection | Check actual first release, configuration/net mass and one Q; released closing FG included once, original return/delivery links retained, no reinspection or dispatch production. Unknown future quantities are open collection duties and cannot be reported as zero. | `yamaha-assembly` |
| validation_full_load | all burden-bearing rows | Verify failed/Q0/start/stop/idle/cleanup/rework load, per-exchange inheritance and only legitimate unassigned closing raw/WIP/non-WIP burden carryover. Do not stock-correct already-net consumption or subtract performed service for held FG. | `ilcd-2010` |
| validation_atom_native | all rows | Every row is one actual material/formulation/article/species/event with correct native extensive property/unit and full restrictions, not a collection. Blank auxiliary identity is disclosed pending matching; no invented UUID, CAS, phase, grade, provider or conversion. Include official Chinese basename where supplied; disclose native identity defects. | `ilcd-2010` |
| validation_species_balance | all physical gates | Independently reconcile total wet physical mass and relevant carbon/nitrogen or other evidenced elements with true species/reaction/free/phase/structural-water evidence. Never fit two unknown quantities using one residual; COD/VOC/NOx/total dust/element readings do not determine a molecule. Gas gross/pass-through/net, integrated volume and actual rate are distinct. Unclosed balance remains review, not a pass. | `ilcd-2010` |
| validation_gross | packaging; assets; service | Audit each six-leg packaging card and each asset/repair/water gross event, full wet stock, same-side-only X terms, one provider owner and one real mixed Waste outlet. No gross leg is scaled by a burden/economic fraction. Raw collection is unnormalised, sole quotient once, no duplicate total/components. | `ilcd-2010` |
| validation_acceptance | manufacture; service | Retain dimensional/finish/assembly, head seating/tension balance, snare engagement/release and acoustic-response inspection to actual specification with inspector/date/disposition. No fixed tension, pitch, holes, life or certification is invented. Rework conformance additionally requires real manufacturer/spec permission and posttests; failed/unapproved work remains in data. | `yamaha-assembly`; `yamaha-tuning`; `yamaha-sound` |
| validation_completeness | dataset | Check every raw field, numerator route, unit conversion, protocol, source applicability, normative context, uncertainty and exclusions. Mechanical projection/finite measurement checks do not approve scientific methodology, translation, supplier or classification mapping. | `ilcd-2010` |

## 10. Published Dataset Profile

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| dataset_role | secondary_dataset; background_dataset |
| downstream_use | Qualified manufacture of the same complete acoustic laminated maple-shell snare and actual observed original-cohort services |
| allowed_use | Actual route/material/component-compatible attributional studies with full limitations and source scope |
| excluded_use | Other instrument families; electrical products; parts-only reference; classification-wide default; future life/performance/certification claim |
| required_metadata | All reference qualifiers; actual supply route and provider boundaries; Q and stock inheritance; raw units; service cohort/horizon; physical gross/source ownership |
| required_quality_disclosure | Identity/provider gaps; measured versus unknown quantities; allocation sensitivity; unclosed balances; actual Q0/reject/rework; evidence limits |
| update_trigger | Material/route/component or native restriction change; changed reference gate; new scientific evidence; observed data outside declared scope |

## 11. Data Sources

| Source id | Type | Reference | Used for |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| yamaha-shell | handbook | https://www.yamaha.com/en/musical_instrument_guide/drums/manufacturing/ | Plywood-to-circular-shell technical route; actual wood, glue, airbag moulding, drying and trim; tom example is not a fixed snare BOM. |
| yamaha-finish | handbook | https://www.yamaha.com/en/musical_instrument_guide/drums/manufacturing/manufacturing002.html | Sanding, separate actual coating stages, UV option, buffing and contrary plastic-wrap route; no recipe or operating settings. |
| yamaha-assembly | handbook | https://www.yamaha.com/en/musical_instrument_guide/drums/manufacturing/manufacturing003.html | Snare bearing edge and bed, drilling, complete assembly and tension-focused final inspection; bass/tom hardware numbers excluded. |
| yamaha-components | handbook | https://www.yamaha.com/en/musical_instrument_guide/drums/mechanism/mechanism002.html | Whole snare anatomy, two heads, counterhoops, lugs, rods, snare and strainer; metal-shell counterexample. |
| yamaha-tuning | handbook | https://www.yamaha.com/en/musical_instrument_guide/drums/play/play004.html | Opposite-pair adjustment principle only; ten-bolt drawing is not mandatory bolt count or tension. |
| yamaha-sound | handbook | https://www.yamaha.com/en/musical_instrument_guide/drums/mechanism/mechanism003.html | Acoustic two-head vibration and tonal adjustment; not a fixed pitch, performance or life claim. |
| yamaha-manual | handbook | https://data.yamaha.com/files/download/other_assets/2/315552/drumset_general.pdf | Complete original multilingual manual; head tightening/conditioning and humidity context, with stands and kit assembly outside reference. |
| yamaha-techniques | handbook | https://www.yamaha.com/US/houseofworship/downloadables/How-to-Choose-Guides/Yamaha-Guide-to-Choosing-a-Drum-Set.pdf | Complete choosing guide; native PDF pp.6–7 alternative shell routes, p.9 snare anatomy and p.10 whole head variants; no universal sizes. |
| remo-head | handbook | https://remo.com/product/ambassador-coated | Independent supplier evidence that a coated film batter head is a supplied complete product; its SKU thickness is not a category default or proof of this factory purchase. |
| ilcd-2010 | official_guidance | https://eplca.jrc.ec.europa.eu/uploads/ILCD-Handbook-General-guide-for-LCA-DETAILED-GUIDANCE-12March2010-ISBN-fin-v1.0-EN.pdf | Attributional subdivision and exchange-specific causal allocation then residual common-gate market value, sections 7.9.2–7.9.3. Bookkeeping/collection below is transparent author method synthesis, not drum manufacturing or numeric evidence. |
